# PKG-FunctionFieldArithmeticPartII

The package is in `packages/FunctionFieldArithmeticPartII/`. The accepted reader has 834 distinct target blocks; `design/plan.json` has 717 nodes. The generated skeleton used the plan's statements, hypotheses, prerequisites, API, tests and locators; the 117 additional explicit reader refinements were retained. Twelve duplicate targets became supplier contracts, leaving 822 numbered local targets. Their API and Checks remain prose; the Lean file is a headline acceptance test.

The named `design/review.json` was absent and the reports directory was empty at intake. I used the accepted review embedded under `review` in `design/plan.json`, alongside its review history and the design reader. Its acceptance concerns the 717-node planning pass, rather than implemented proofs. I did not infer an implementation claim for the reader's additional refinements.

## Headline inventory, listed before writing Lean

All Lean names below are in `TauCetiRoadmap.FunctionFieldArithmeticPartII`.

| Headline | Acceptance status |
| --- | --- |
| Sharp affine root torsor comparison over every commutative ring at every positive exponent | Stated: `rootChartComparison_kernel`, `rootChartComparison_cokernel`, `rootChartComparison_injective_iff`, `rootChartComparison_surjective_iff`, `rootChartComparisonMatrix_det`. Four simple definitions give actual quotient algebras, their left coaction, their tensor comparison and its monomial matrix. |
| Relative regular finite-root geometry, tame DM and wild inertia distinctions | Not statable at the pins: native finite-root stack carrier and its geometric morphism predicates are missing. |
| Coherent infinite root stack, fpqc quotient, factorial cofinality and arbitrary base change | Not statable at the pins: coherent root pseudofunctor and represented band-action quotient are missing. |
| Infinite affine coinvariants in rational-character coordinates | Not statable with a few simple definitions: the actual root-colimit coaction and its rational-character grading require the substantial constructions specified in Layer 3. No arbitrary coaction is substituted. |
| Infinite DVR reduced gerbe, fpqc Kummer classification and a non-fppf-locally-trivial torsor | Not statable at the pins: relative infinite gerbe and fpqc torsor groupoid carriers are missing. A number-field root-existence statement would not replace this theorem. |
| Root Picard square quotient and signed adelic double-action equivalence | Not statable at the pins: graded root Picard stack and completed-lattice gluing functor are missing. |
| Symmetric lisse lines across collisions and multiplicativity | Not statable at the pins: root symmetric-power stack and its adic lisse coefficient category are missing. |
| Exterior-power cohomology and its Koszul invariant input | The linear comparison is stated as `exteriorPower_koszul_invariants`, for arbitrary modules over arbitrary commutative rings with unit factorial. The geometric cohomology theorem is not statable: stack étale cohomology and Frobenius on these coefficients are missing. |
| High-degree Abel–Jacobi effective descent with the uniform bound | Not statable at the pins: root Abel–Jacobi morphism and stack lisse descent are missing. |
| Unique normalized character sheaf in every integer degree | Not statable at the pins: graded root Picard stack and its lisse category with Frobenius are missing. |
| Exact ramified norm sheaf complex, based torsor fibres and cohomological obstructions | Not statable at the pins: root multiplicative sheaf, root norm and compatible torsor groupoids are missing. |
| Quadratic all-degree Frobenius trace equals the idele character | Not statable at the pins: root Picard character sheaf and its Frobenius trace carrier are missing. |

The file contains ten declarations: four definitions and six headline theorems. There are no admitted data, extra imports, option overrides, API lemmas or admitted examples.

## Corrections and support boundaries

- The arithmetic supplier uses `b ↦ O(E(b))`, whereas this design uses `a ↦ O(−E(a))`. The exact contract now uses `b=a⁻¹`, including inversion of the rigidification. Over 𝔽₅, a=2 gives b=3, whose inverse rigidification is 2. An inverse uniformizer here becomes the supplier's uniformizer and both yield O(x). This was checked against FunctionFieldArithmetic §4.8 at its open head.
- Obsolete SF/FA/IG stage labels were replaced by actual section headings. SchemeAndStackFoundations §§3.3–3.7 supplies the ordinary curve/Picard interfaces; FunctionFieldArithmetic's relevant arithmetic sections are §2.1 and §§4.1–4.10, rather than historical FA identifiers.
- The finite root definition retained locally is explicitly the extension to algebraic-stack bases; the supplied scheme-base root is imported. Native framed and section-coordinate comparisons remain different from the generic root construction.
- Tensor/exterior projection, factorial composites, antisymmetrizer composites, naturality and ordinary finite-group annihilation are imports at the actual Tau Ceti pin. The old reader's claim that these imports postdate the compilation pin was removed. The signed invariant-image theorem remains local.
- The projective-space simple-connectivity locator is SGA1 line 8606, Exposé XI, Proposition 1.1, printed p.285; its hyperplane proof uses Exposé X, Corollaire 2.10. A generic recollection of Exposé X is not used as the endpoint locator.
- The positive-exponent comparison theorems keep an explicit `0 < n`. At n=0, A=ℤ, f=2, the parameter chart is zero, the unity chart is ℤ[T], and both comparison tensor algebras are zero. The comparison is bijective although 0=1 or 2 is a unit is false. The negative-control Check says “source and target tensor algebras”, not “both charts”.
- The determinant includes its pair-index permutation sign. At n=2, f=2 it is −2; at n=3 it is 8; at n=4 it is 64. No exponent-invertibility restriction was added.
- The source's reduced branch gerbes, unshifted/perverse notation, apparent quadratic-only monodromy in the proof of A.8, nonrepresentable ordered maps, quadratic fibre coordinates and Picard “exactness” are qualified by the accepted design's explicit replacements. These were kept: full nilpotent fibres; L_d[d] perverse; arbitrary rank-one global monodromy; proper quasi-finite stack maps; retained (u,α) coordinates; and based norm fibres with H² obstructions. In particular the source's Picard display is not asserted as exactness of ordinary Picard groups.

The README cannot support claims of implemented geometry from the inherited proof receipts, historical code archives or older admitted catalogues. Those process claims and evidence-source aliases were eliminated. The mathematics and its intended interfaces remain targets. Talpo–Vistoli's logarithmic statements retain their fine/saturated setting; the all-ring affine formulas here are direct quotient-and-character deductions, not claims that the source states every such formula over arbitrary nilpotent parameters.

Bresciani's B24 was classified by the source service as `uncertified`: only a paywalled location resolved. It is cited by its locators, as authorized. No source text is requested, no settled citation is reopened, and no needs-references file is pending.

## Ownership sweep and imports

I read the shapes of AlgebraicVectorBundles, JacobianChallenge and ClassFieldTheory on main before rendering. Ownership searches used the required `roadmaps grep/show` tools over main and all indexed open heads, plus the board package tree and the pinned source trees. Search terms covered root triples, diagonalizable quotients, infinite roots, root Picard stacks, symmetric local systems, signed tensor invariants, tensor/exterior maps, torsor towers, completed lattices, norms and character traces. The native declarations remain in this roadmap's own namespace, distinct from readable supplier roadmaps.

The substantive supplier files were refreshed after the tools updated their main index. SchemeAndStackFoundations, AlgebraicVectorBundles, AlgebraicCurves, JacobianChallenge, EtaleCohomologyFoundations and ProfiniteCohomology matched their local main texts. FunctionFieldArithmetic's refreshed open-head text was identical to the text used for its contracts. RepresentationTheory/SchurWeyl was read at its main-path copy: its open revision is not a dependency. The resolver found no unresolved roadmap citation. Its own pinned-library check was unavailable, so it was supplemented with direct filesystem checks at the actual pins.

The further tensor/exterior identities in the antisymmetrization subsection are explicitly imported from the pinned declarations, while the assertion that the map lands in the signed invariants remains its local target. The finite normalizations, finite coefficient diagrams, relative comparisons, infinite towers and ramified geometric character construction have no competing owner at the checked sources: the general supplier theories do not specify these root coordinates or coherent infinite objects.

## Notions moved below their consumers

The design mentioned later fundamental-group and duality roadmaps and an unregistered stack-sheaf successor. The package instead imports the now-main scheme contracts from EtaleCohomologyFoundations and owns the necessary stack extensions in Layers 7–8:

- Normal DM-stack finite-cover monodromy, inertia and lisse atlas descent.
- Smooth Artin-stack lisse coefficient descent, tensor/Hom and finite-level proper degree-zero base change.
- Nonrepresentable ordered-map pushforward, rational coarse stalk comparison, nilpotent invariance and Leray, with the shifted intermediate-extension comparison.
- Scheme projective-space simple connectivity at every algebraically closed field and every nonnegative dimension.

No later roadmap is named as an input to these targets. The generic tensor, ordinary curve and ordinary scheme cohomology theories retain their suppliers.

## Supplier gaps

Only the top-level `gaps` list in `design/plan.json` changed; a mechanical comparison after deleting that key gave equality of the two complete JSON trees. Historical mixed obligation records were replaced by the following exact supplier gaps, also stated under the corresponding supplier's README subsection:

- finite-diagonalizable-coarse: SchemeAndStackFoundations 1.23. Extend §1.23’s affine finite-group coarse quotient contract to finite locally free diagonalizable group schemes, including nonreduced ones, with invariants and arbitrary base-change compatibility.
- invertible-section-coordinates: AlgebraicVectorBundles L0A–L0B. Identify its coherent tensor-section, free-unit and pullback maps with native invertible-sheaf coordinates, so that tensor sections evaluate to multiplication and the unit evaluates to one.
- completed-lattice-root-dictionary: AlgebraicCurves Layer 12. Compare completed local lattices and their residue root frames with rationally framed line bundles, preserving weighted degrees and the sign π_x⁻¹↦O_X(x).
- quadratic-root-residue-character: FunctionFieldArithmetic 4.1–4.5; 4.8–4.10. Specialize its local Artin and global norm quotient to quadratic covers in odd characteristic, identifying the ramified unit character with the residue-square character and the inverse-uniformizer value away from ramification with geometric Frobenius.
- coherent-site-universe-lifts: SchemeAndStackFoundations 1.18. Provide coherent universe enlargement of the scheme site, native invertible sheaves, sections, tensor products and pullback, restricted to the essential image of the smaller site.

## Pins, source receipts and elaboration

The content manifest pins Mathlib at `6b7abb3c7686292736be2955bd3eb9ebf63b456a` and Tau Ceti at `a91d3aafa8cd3e6bc33dfde0d7677ed0f1625039`. The design's older pins were not substituted for them. All 347 inherited baseline module paths exist at these pins. Token-location checks found 342 directly; the five apostrophe-ending names were false negatives of the word-boundary pattern and were checked directly in their named files. Important defining bodies and assumptions were read: native polynomial quotient lifts and root relation; tensor-product algebra maps and lift; exterior projection and both composites; finite-group transfer; finite-root and Picard supplier definitions. A source-token hit alone was not used to infer missing geometric structures.

`lake env lean` was run from the content checkout on the absolute target corresponding to `packages/FunctionFieldArithmeticPartII/Suggested.lean`. Exit status: 0. Exactly **6** warnings, all “warning: declaration uses `sorry`”. Errors: 0. Other messages: none. No Tau Ceti object-file build was needed because the file uses only `import Mathlib`.

A separate scratch copy added `#check @name` for all ten declarations and elaborated successfully. I compared all ten elaborated types to the README: `CommRing A`, the arbitrary parameter and exponent, positivity in every sharp comparison statement, module and commutative-ring hypotheses for the signed theorem, unit factorial, and both independent universes were retained. No binder was silently dropped. Definitions carry their own binders. The tensor map has the intended source and target; the annihilator is the kernel of left multiplication by f; the cokernel is a module quotient by the actual comparison range. No signature change was needed after that binder comparison.

The final sources manifest has 36 identities: 35 certified legal retrievals and one settled uncertified citation. It preserves the service's URL, fetched-from URL, timestamp, hash, licence classification and status fields; no extraction or source passage was copied into the package. Main geometric passages read include AGV08 lines 2404–2478; TV17 lines 630–784; YZ19 lines 6912–7940; Alper's root examples at FramedRoot-codex-rtOQ9t line 7989; AOV08 line 906; the scheme limits, torsors, norm and descent tags; SGA1 line 8606; and the supporting transfer/exterior and adic-coefficient locators. ## Adversarial computations

The finite-instance pass below gives a row for every retained target and every displayed API assertion. The rows use shared explicit models when several projection, naturality or coherence assertions concern the same maps. The entries record values, not just a verdict. These are sanity computations, not substitutes for proofs of the general theorems. Every construction retains its complete source/target, and assumptions and negative controls are also expressed in the README.

Independent coefficient-matrix calculations checked n=1,2,3,4,5 and f=−2,0,1,2 over ℤ (20 determinant comparisons). Exhaustive n=2 finite-ring coefficient maps gave:

| Ring and parameter | Kernel cardinality | Image cardinality | Cokernel cardinality |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 𝔽₂, f=0 | 2 | 8 | 2 |
| 𝔽₂, f=1 | 1 | 16 | 1 |
| 𝔽₃, f=0 | 3 | 27 | 3 |
| 𝔽₃, f=1 | 1 | 81 | 1 |
| ℤ/4, f=2 | 2 | 128 | 2 |

The zero-ring case has one element in each module and every proposed isomorphism is the unique map. The ℤ, n=2, f=2 case has determinant −2, zero kernel and cokernel ℤ/2; thus injectivity and surjectivity require different conditions. At n=0 the source and target tensor algebras are zero for f=2, explaining the explicit positivity hypothesis.

| Statement or convention | Instances tried | Computed values on the two routes | Result | Change |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| Coframe sign | n=2; β′=−β | β′(t)=−β(t); zero section has both labels ±1 | Geometric coordinates do not erase inertia | Retained |
